## Page 68

4 ZTNA
#x≥VIP
KIENGFWSZTNA
Gartnerx†ZTNAHJÆX
Zero trust network access (ZTNA) is a product or service that creates an
identity- and context-based, logical access boundary around an application or set
of applications. The applications are hidden from discovery, and access is restricted
via a trust broker to a set of named entities. The broker verifies the identity,
context and policy adherence of the specified participants before allowing access
and prohibits lateral movement elsewhere in the network. This removes application
assets from public visibility and significantly reduces the surface area for attack.

SAML2.0
Security Assertion Markupl Language =#=$=| (SAML 2.0 is one
of the most widely used open standard for authentication and authorizing
between múłtíple bares 80
It's one of the protocol that give users the single sign-on (SSO) experience for
applications[» APPSISSO]. The other adopted open standard is OAuth and
Open|D[2021*27##]. Of the two, SAML 2.0, released in 2005, remains the 800
pound gorilla in Enterprise SSO space.
At its core, Security Assertion Markup Language (SAML) 2.0 is a means to
exchange authorization and authentication information|&*ÌES]
between services. SAML is frequently used to implement internal corporate single
sign-on (SSO) solutions where the user logs into a service that acts as the single
source of identity which then grants access to a subset of other internal services.

your network and add the proxy to one or

network must configure their browsers to

under Network > Explicit Proxy. Create

to proxy traffic.
